TL;DR: 本文回顾了自然语言生成（NLG）评估从1990年至今的发展历程，指出评估方法从早期与语言学紧密结合、缺乏现代实验评估，发展到2026年与机器学习深度融合、以实验评估为核心，并预测未来影响评估、定性评估和安全评估将变得日益重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳理NLG评估方法在过去三十余年间的演变轨迹，并展望未来发展方向，以应对NLG技术被大规模日常使用的现实需求。

Method: 采用历史回顾与趋势分析的方法，总结1990年至2026年间NLG评估技术的演变，特别关注LLM-as-Judge等最新发展，并基于技术应用现状对未来趋势进行预测。

Result: 识别出NLG评估从语言学导向到机器学习导向的范式转变，指出LLM-as-Judge是最近的代表性评估技术，并预测影响评估、定性评估和安全评估将成为未来重点。

Conclusion: NLG评估将持续演化，随着NLG技术被大规模公众日常使用，影响评估、定性评估和安全评估的重要性将显著提升。

Abstract: Natural Language Generation (NLG) evaluation has changed dramatically since 1990, and will continue to evolve in the future. In 1990, when NLG had close ties to linguistics, there was very little formal experimental evaluation in the modern sense. In 2026, when NLG is closely linked to machine learning, experimental evaluation is expected and indeed fundamental to research. Many evaluation techniques were developed over this period, including most recently LLM-as-Judge. I expect NLG evaluation will continue to evolve in the future. In particular, impact, qualitative, and safety evaluation will become more important as large numbers of people routinely use NLG technology.

</details>


### [109] [Is a Document Educational or Just Wikipedia-Style? -- Pitfalls of Classifier-Based Quality Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23721)
*Mateusz Klimaszewski,Piotr Andruszkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 分类器质量过滤（CQF）在预训练语料构建中虽有效，但研究发现简单的维基百科式格式重排操作可显著改变模型的质量评估，使低质量内容绕过过滤阈值。FineWeb-Edu CQF模型约7%的文档因此被反转过滤决策，将原本应排除的内容引入预训练语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示基于分类器的质量过滤方法存在安全漏洞，即格式变换可操纵模型评估结果，使低质量内容混入预训练语料。

Method: 对文档执行维基百科风格的重格式化操作，观察FineWeb-Edu CQF模型的质量评估变化及过滤决策反转比例。

Result: 约7%的评估文档过滤决策被反转，低质量内容因此被错误地纳入预训练语料。

Conclusion: 当前分类器质量过滤方法存在格式敏感性漏洞，需警惕对抗性格式变换对过滤机制的规避风险。

Abstract: Classifier-based Quality Filtering has recently emerged as a fundamental technique in constructing pre-training corpora. The ability to deploy a single model that can replace or supplement a set of heuristics has proven effective across numerous Large Language Models. In this work, we expose a critical vulnerability in this approach by demonstrating how a straightforward Wikipedia-style reformatting operation can substantially alter a model's quality assessment and enable low-quality content to surpass filtering thresholds. Our analysis reveals that the FineWeb-Edu CQF model would reverse its filtering decision for approximately 7% of evaluated documents, thereby admitting content into the pre-training corpus that would otherwise have been excluded.

</details>


### [110] [Hierarchical Concept Geometry in Language Models Emerges from Word Co-occurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23821)
*Andres Nava,Matthieu Wyart*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布理论，证明词嵌入（word2vec）中上下位关系（hypernymy）的层级几何结构源于共现统计的谱性质，并在Gemma 2B模型中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释上下位关系（"is-a"关系）如何在语言表示的几何空间中编码，探究这种层级结构是否来自特定的功能性机制，还是可以仅从词共现统计中涌现。

Method: 基于WordNet超词图上的距离与共现频率的负相关假设，在共现核满足正性和衰减条件下，理论上刻画了word2vec嵌入的Gram矩阵谱结构，证明前导特征向量先分离大分类分支、再逐步细分，形成"由粗到细"的层级分裂几何。在word2vec和Gemma 2B的unembedding上进行了实验验证。

Result: 理论预测与word2vec在多个WordNet子树的嵌入一致，该层级谱特征在Gemma 2B的unembedding中也显著存在。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中层级概念几何不一定需要专门的层级功能机制，而是可以从成对词统计的谱结构中自然涌现。

Abstract: We propose a distributional theory of how hypernymy -- the ``is-a'' relation between general and specific concepts -- is encoded geometrically in language representations. Starting from the empirically verified assumption that words closer on the WordNet hypernym graph co-occur more often, we characterize theoretically the spectrum of the resulting embedding Gram matrix of word2vec embeddings. Under mild positivity and decay conditions on the co-occurrence kernel, we prove that the leading eigenvectors first separate broad taxonomic branches and then progressively finer sub-branches, producing a \emph{hierarchical splitting geometry} with a coarse-to-fine spectral organization that mirrors the tree. We confirm these predictions in word2vec embeddings across many sampled WordNet subtrees, and show that the same signature extends strikingly well to Gemma 2B unembeddings. Our results indicate that hierarchical concept geometry in LLMs need not reflect a hierarchy-specific functional mechanism, but emerges from the spectral structure of pairwise word statistics.

</details>


### [111] [Multilingual Knowledge Transfer under Data Constraints via Lexical Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23885)
*Anastasiia Sedova,Natalie Schluter,Skyler Seto,Maartje ter Hoeve*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LINK方法，通过在预训练数据中对高资源语言（英语）部分进行词汇替换（使用双语词汇表将随机选择的词替换为其翻译），以数据级干预方式提升跨语言知识迁移，无需平行数据、翻译系统或额外训练阶段，在8种语言和5种模型规模上验证了有效性，最高可实现2倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言训练数据不足，而现有跨语言知识迁移方法依赖大量平行数据、翻译系统、辅助模型或额外训练阶段，这些资源对许多语言难以获取，亟需一种低成本、轻量级的知识迁移方法。

Method: LINK是一种数据级干预方法：在预训练阶段，对高资源语言（英语）语料中的部分数据，按给定替换比例随机选择单词，使用双语词汇表将其替换为对应的目标语言翻译词，无需额外模型训练，仅需一个几乎零成本获取的双语词汇表。

Result: 在8种语言和5种模型规模上的实验表明，LINK在目标语言的下游任务中取得了显著改进，最高可实现2倍的训练加速以达到等价的性能水平。

Conclusion: LINK通过简单的数据级词汇替换策略，以极低资源代价有效提升了跨语言知识迁移效果，尤其适用于缺乏平行数据和翻译系统的低资源语言场景。

Abstract: Cross-lingual knowledge transfer is critical for building high-performing multilingual language models for languages with insufficient training data. When target language data is scarce, the knowledge required for many downstream tasks involving scientific reasoning, commonsense inference, and world knowledge must be acquired primarily from the high-resource language, making effective knowledge transfer essential. Existing methods for improving such cross-lingual knowledge transfer require large amounts of parallel data, translation systems, auxiliary models, or additional training stages that are largely unavailable for many languages. We propose LINK - a data-level intervention method that improves knowledge transfer during model pretraining through lexical substitutions in high-resource part of pretraining data using bilingual vocabularies. For a given replacement ratio, randomly selected words in a portion of the high-resource (English) training corpus are swapped with their word-level translations, requiring no additional model training and only a bilingual vocabulary, which can be obtained at near-zero cost for virtually any language. Evaluation on eight languages across five model sizes shows notable improvements on downstream tasks in the target language, with up to a 2x speedup in training to reach equivalent performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [BOHM: Zero-Cost Hierarchical Attribution for Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22866)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BOHM是一种从路由权重直接提取层级归因树的方法，零边际成本、无需访问组件内部，可同时提供多分辨率归因。与SHAP相比，BOHM回答不同的问题，在路由接近最优时两者收敛。实验表明BOHM在多种场景下与SHAP高度相关（tau最高0.980），且满足效率、单调性、对称性和弱抑制性，但不满足Shapley的可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley-based归因方法（如SHAP）需要评估系统在任意组件子集上的表现，这在第三方API、不透明端点以及将路由集中到少数工具的智能编排系统中无法实现，因为这些系统不支持对任意组件子集进行评估。

Method: BOHM从系统已有的路由权重中提取层级归因树：叶节点归因是根到叶路径权重的乘积；第k层归因是深度k节点上的诱导分布。该方法零边际成本，无需访问组件内部，可同时提供多分辨率归因。

Result: 1) 在18个LLM的3层层级、880个LiveCodeBench问题上，BOHM的Kendall tau=0.928，SHAP在每种子9000倍推理下达到tau=0.980；2) 在5个驱动、7个基准的智能体研究中，驱动将路由集中在单一工具上（top-share中位数0.65），BOHM与SHAP的一致性由驱动选择的工具是否为经验最优预测；3) 在475叶、4层的美国人口普查层级上，BOHM在各层恢复真实排名（tau最高0.722）。

Conclusion: BOHM应被理解为一种互补的归因基元：一种在路由状态存在处即可计算的多分辨率分解方法，其与Shapley的不一致性本身具有诊断价值。BOHM满足效率、单调性、对称性和弱抑制性，但不满足Shapley的可加性。

Abstract: Compound AI systems route tasks through hierarchies of specialised components. Attribution is dominated by Shapley-based methods (SHAP), which decompose a coalition value function into per-component marginal contributions and require evaluation of the system on arbitrary component subsets. That requirement fails for third-party APIs, opaque endpoints, and agentic orchestrators that concentrate routing on a few tools, leaving most coalitions un-evaluable from the deployed orchestrator. We introduce BOHM, which extracts a hierarchical attribution tree directly from the routing weights such systems already maintain: leaf attribution is the path product of root-to-leaf routing weights; level-k attribution is the induced distribution over depth-k nodes. The method has zero marginal cost, requires no access to component internals, and provides multi-resolution attribution at every level simultaneously, which flat methods cannot offer at any evaluation budget. BOHM and SHAP answer different questions and converge when the deployed router routes near-optimally. On 18 LLMs in a 3-level hierarchy over 880 LiveCodeBench problems, BOHM yields Kendall tau=0.928; SHAP reaches tau=0.980 at 9,000x more coalition evaluations per seed. On a 5-driver, 7-benchmark agentic study (35 cells, complete coverage), drivers concentrate routing on a single tool (top-share median 0.65), and cell-level tau(BOHM,SHAP) is predicted by whether the driver's top pick is the empirically best tool (mean +0.22 vs ~+0.01). On a US Census hierarchy (475 leaves, 4 levels), BOHM recovers ground-truth rankings at every level (tau up to 0.722). BOHM satisfies efficiency, monotonicity, symmetry, and weak suppression but not Shapley's additivity. It is best understood as a complementary primitive: a multi-resolution decomposition computable wherever routing state exists, whose disagreement with Shapley is itself diagnostic.

</details>


### [113] [NeuroNL2LTL: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Natural Language Translation of Linear Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22874)
*Paapa Kwesi Quansah,Ernest Bonnah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuroNL2LTL 是一个神经符号架构，通过将自然语言到LTL的翻译路由到结构保持的中间表示，并结合可满足性检查、最小编辑修复机制以及验证器参与训练的强化学习，实现了高可靠性的形式化规范生成。在20万+跨领域需求上，语义等价率达28%，86%输出可满足，并生成可解释性说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言到形式逻辑（如LTL）的翻译需要专业知识，限制了形式化验证在安全关键开发中的普及。现有模板方法牺牲表达力换取可靠性，神经方法虽流畅但缺乏正确性保证。

Method: 提出神经符号架构NeuroNL2LTL：(1) 通过结构保持的中间表示将NL映射到LTL；(2) 生成规范后进行可满足性和非平凡性检查；(3) 最小编辑修复机制纠正接近正确的输出；(4) 核心创新：将验证结果作为强化学习奖励信号，使神经组件直接优化形式正确性。

Result: 在20万+跨领域需求（航空航天、机器人、自动驾驶等10+领域）上，语义等价率28%，86%输出可验证为可满足的，同时生成上下文可解释性说明供领域专家验证。

Conclusion: 形式化验证可作为神经规范系统的训练目标和运行时过滤器，使得基于神经的工具能够从逻辑保证而不是统计置信度获得可靠性。

Abstract: Effectively translating between natural language (NL) and formal logics like Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) requires expertise that limits formal verification's reach in safety-critical development. Template-based approaches sacrifice expressiveness for reliability; neural methods achieve fluency but provide no correctness guarantees. We present NeuroNL2LTL, a neurosymbolic architecture unifying learned translation with formal verification. NeuroNL2LTL routes translation through an intermediate representation whose mapping to LTL is structure-preserving by construction. Generated specifications undergo satisfiability and non-triviality checking; a minimal-edit repair mechanism corrects near-miss outputs before they reach downstream tools. The central innovation is verifier-in-the-loop training: verification outcomes serve as reward signals for reinforcement learning, producing neural components that optimize directly for formal correctness. On 200,000+ requirements spanning aerospace, robotics, autonomous vehicles, and ten additional domains, NeuroNL2LTL achieves 28\% semantic equivalence with reference specifications while ensuring 86\% of outputs are verified satisfiable. The system also generates contextually grounded explanations from LTL, enabling domain experts to validate specifications without specialized training. This work demonstrates that formal verification can function as both training objective and runtime filter for neural specification systems, allowing us to build neural-based tools whose reliability derives from logical guarantees rather than statistical confidence.

</details>


### [114] [RMA: an Agentic System for Research-Level Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22875)
*Zelin Zhao,Bo Yuan,Jaemoo Choi,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了研究数学智能体（RMA），一个用于自动化推理研究级数学问题的智能体框架。该框架将证明求解分解为多个专业模块，通过多角色多轮协作工作流生成、精炼和验证候选证明。在First Proof基准测试中，RMA解决了10个研究问题中的8个，优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要集中在竞赛数学或形式化定理证明，缺乏针对需要长程推理、文献支撑和迭代证明精化的研究级数学问题的自动化推理框架。

Method: RMA将研究级证明求解分解为问题分析、文献搜索与理解、公平比较、知识库构建和证明验证等专业模块，由初始化器、提议器和验证器三个智能体通过共享结构化内存进行多角色多轮协作，迭代生成、精炼和验证候选证明。

Result: 在First Proof基准测试上，RMA解决了10个研究问题中的8个，优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等基线模型，生成的证明在逻辑合理性和可读性上更优。消融实验表明性能提升源于结构化推理模块、迭代精炼和验证器反馈的协同作用。

Conclusion: RMA框架有效解决了研究级数学问题的自动化推理挑战，其多模块多智能体协作的设计方法显著优于现有方法，为将AI推理能力从竞赛数学扩展到前沿数学研究提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present $\textbf{Research Math Agents (RMA)}$, an agentic framework for automated reasoning on research-level mathematical problems. Unlike prior studies centered on competition mathematics or formal theorem proving, RMA targets research-level mathematical problems that require long-horizon reasoning, literature grounding, and iterative proof refinement. RMA decomposes research-level proof solving into specialized modules for problem analysis, literature search and understanding, fair comparison, knowledge-bank construction, and proof verification, all coordinated by initializer, proposer, and verifier agents through a shared structured memory. Within this unified framework, these agents operate in a multi-role, multi-round workflow, collaboratively generating, refining, and verifying candidate proofs through iterative feedback. We evaluate RMA on the First Proof benchmark, which consists of ten research-level problems contributed by expert mathematicians across diverse domains. Through comprehensive expert evaluation, RMA outperforms strong baselines on the First Proof benchmark, including GPT-5.2R and Aletheia, solving eight out of ten research problems and producing more logically sound and readable proofs. Our comprehensive ablation studies further show that performance gains arise from the interaction of structured reasoning modules, iterative refinement, and verifier-based feedback, rather than any single component. Our solutions and implementations will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [115] [SciAtlas: A Large-Scale Knowledge Graph for Automated Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22878)
*Shuofei Qiao,Yunxiang Wei,Jiazheng Fan,Bin Wu,Busheng Zhang,Mengru Wang,Yuqi Zhu,Ningyu Zhang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciAtlas是一个覆盖26个学科、4300万篇论文、1.57亿实体和30亿三元组的大规模多学科异质学术资源知识图谱，并配套设计了神经符号检索算法（三路径协同召回+图重排序），在降低推理成本的同时实现从语义匹配到确定性关联发现的跨越，可作为AI Agent的"认知地图"支撑文献综述、趋势综合、创意定位等全流程自动化科学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全球学术产出呈指数级增长，导致"信息爆炸"问题，知识组织碎片化且非结构化，阻碍了跨学科深度整合。现有学术检索工具主要依赖浅层关键词匹配或向量空间语义检索，缺乏处理复杂逻辑连接的拓扑推理能力；而基于深度研究框架的Agent方法容易出现逻辑幻觉且推理成本高昂。

Method: 本文构建了SciAtlas大规模多学科异质学术资源知识图谱，整合了26个学科、超过4300万篇论文、1.57亿实体和30亿三元组。在此基础上，开发了一种神经符号检索算法，包含三路径协同召回（tri-path collaborative recall）和图重排序（graph reranking），实现了从简单语义匹配到确定性关联发现的平滑过渡。

Result: SciAtlas提供了结构化的拓扑认知基板，打破了学科壁垒，为AI Agent提供了全局视角。该框架在文献综述、自动化研究趋势综合、创意定位和学术轨迹探索等关键应用方向上展现了有效性，能够作为"认知地图"赋能全流程自动化科学研究，同时显著降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: SciAtlas作为一种大规模学术知识图谱及其配套的神经符号检索算法，有效解决了学术信息爆炸和跨学科整合困难的问题，在降低推理成本的同时提升了AI Agent在学术研究中的逻辑推理能力，可作为自动化科学研究的核心基础设施。相关接口已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: The exponential growth of global academic output has confronted researchers and AI agents with an unprecedented ``information explosion,'' where fragmented and unstructured knowledge organization impedes deep interdisciplinary integration. Current academic retrieval tools predominantly rely on superficial keyword matching or vector-space semantic retrieval, which lack the topological reasoning capabilities required to navigate complex logical connections. Agentic deep-research-based frameworks are often prone to logical hallucinations and consuming high inference costs. To bridge this gap, in this report, we introduce SciAtlas, a large-scale, multi-disciplinary, heterogeneous academic resource knowledge graph designed as a panoramic scientific evolution network. By integrating over 43M papers from 26 disciplines, and a total of 157M entities and 3B triplets, SciAtlas provides a structured topological cognitive substrate that dismantles disciplinary barriers and furnishes AI agents with a global perspective. Furthermore, we develop a neuro-symbolic retrieval algorithm featuring tri-path collaborative recall and graph reranking, achieving a seamless transition from simple semantic matching to deterministic association discovery. We also present key application directions of SciAtlas, including literature review, automated research trend synthesis, idea positioning, and academic trajectory exploration, to demonstrate that SciAtlas can serve as an effective ``cognitive map'' to empower the full loop of automated scientific research while significantly reducing reasoning costs. We have released the interfaces for KG retrieval and various downstream tasks in our GitHub repo.

</details>


### [116] [ImProver 2: Iteratively Self-Improving LMs for Neurosymbolic Proof Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22885)
*Riyaz Ahuja,Tate Rowney,Jeremy Avigad,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ImProver 2 是一个神经符号框架，用于在 Lean 4 中自动优化形式化证明。它结合了数据高效的专家迭代流程和暴露形式结构及轻量级非正式抽象的脚手架。训练出的 7B 参数模型在结构证明指标上超越了同系列的大模型，并能与中档前沿模型竞争，验证了证明优化作为可扩展、可学习任务的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学库快速增长，验证过的证明需要重构以保持可维护性并提升神经证明器的训练数据质量。但可扩展的证明优化受到异构且启发式指定的目标、数据稀缺以及高昂的训练和推理成本的阻碍。

Method: 提出 ImProver 2 框架，包含数据高效的专家迭代流程（expert-iteration pipeline）和一个神经符号脚手架（neurosymbolic scaffold），该脚手架既暴露形式化结构，又引入轻量级非正式抽象。同时引入一套度量标准来捕获结构化的证明属性。

Result: 使用 ImProver 2 训练的 7B 参数模型在性能上超越同系列中数个数量级更大的模型，与中档前沿模型竞争力相当。神经符号脚手架显著提升了小型和前沿模型的性能。小型模型在复杂多样的度量上能够有效重构研究级证明，匹配更大系统的表现。

Conclusion: 证明优化是可扩展、可学习的任务。通过合适的脚手架和训练，小型模型可以在形式化证明重构上达到与更大系统相当的水平，无需依赖超大模型。

Abstract: Formal mathematics libraries are rapidly expanding, creating a growing need to refactor verified proofs for maintainability and to improve training data quality for neural provers. However, scalable proof optimization is hindered by heterogeneous and heuristically specified objectives, scarce data, and high training and inference costs. To overcome these challenges, we introduce ImProver 2, a neurosymbolic framework for automated proof optimization in Lean 4. ImProver 2 combines a data-efficient expert-iteration pipeline with a scaffold that exposes formal structure alongside lightweight informal abstractions. We further introduce a suite of metrics capturing structural proof properties. Using ImProver 2, we train a 7B-parameter model that outperforms orders-of-magnitude larger models within the same model family, and is competitive with mid-tier frontier models across metrics. We additionally demonstrate that our neurosymbolic scaffold significantly improves performance across both small and frontier models. We show that with proper scaffolding and training, small models can effectively restructure research-level proofs over complex and varied metrics, matching substantially larger systems and establishing proof optimization as a scalable, learnable task.

</details>


### [117] [EVE-Agent: Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22905)
*Yamato Arai,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVE-Agent通过证据可验证机制（边际准确率增益）替代不可靠的自生成训练数据，在不对基础模型、检索器或优化框架做任何修改的情况下，显著提升了自演化搜索智能体的证据溯源正确性，使训练课程可审计、可信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化智能体在无人类标注的数据自由场景中，容易因缺乏可验证证据而陷入"流畅但无依据"的自我增强循环，导致训练信号不透明且不可靠。作者认为证据可验证性是可信自演化的先决条件。

Method: 基于proposer-solver框架的改进：Proposer生成问题、答案及逐字证据片段；证据验证器通过比较提供证据前后的边际准确率增益来奖励该证据，由此产生偏好证据真正有助于回答问题的训练信号，无需oracle答案、人工标签或外部标注。

Result: EVE-Agent在证据溯源正确性上显著优于先前的自演化搜索智能体；生成的训练课程不仅自生成，而且每个训练实例都带有可审查的源证据片段，解释了为何该实例值得信任。

Conclusion: 证据可验证性是实现可信自演化搜索智能体的关键原则；EVE-Agent在不修改基础模型、检索器、搜索工具和优化框架的前提下，通过边际证据增益机制实现了更可靠的自我改进。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents should not train on examples they cannot justify. Data-free self-evolving search agents offer a scalable route to systems that generate their own questions, answer them, and improve from their own feedback without human annotations. Yet, without verifiable evidence, this loop can reward fluent but unsupported examples, turning the self-generated curriculum into an opaque and potentially unreliable training signal. We argue that evidence verifiability is a prerequisite for trustworthy self-evolution in search agents: each generated instance should include not only an answer but also a source-grounded span whose contribution to that answer can be measured. We introduce EVE-Agent, an Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agent that operationalizes this principle through a modification to the proposer--solver framework. The proposer generates a question, an answer, and a verbatim evidence span. An evidence verifier then rewards the span according to the marginal accuracy gain when the evidence is provided. This produces a training signal that favors evidence that genuinely helps answer the question, without requiring oracle answers, human labels, or external annotations. EVE-Agent leaves the backbone model, retriever, search tool, and optimization framework unchanged. Experiments show that EVE-Agent substantially improves evidence-grounded correctness over prior self-evolving search agents. The resulting curriculum is not merely self-generated but auditable by construction: each training example carries an inspectable source span that explains why it should be trusted.

</details>


### [118] [GENSTRAT: Toward a Science of Strategic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23238)
*Vartan Shadarevian,Kia Ghods,Alex Kenich,Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GENSTRAT：一个通过程序化生成策略环境（两玩家零和、不完美信息卡牌游戏）来评估LLM作为经济智能体的框架，包含能力剖面（六轴分解）和锯齿度指标，发现整体强度相近的模型在能力分布和局部稳定性上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有战略推理基准使用固定规范博弈，难以泛化到真实部署中复杂多变的策略环境；且基准可能因模型进步而饱和，无法持续评估。

Method: 提出GENSTRAT框架，包含：(1) 程序化生成器，可从分布中按需生成两玩家零和不完美信息卡牌游戏；(2) 六轴能力剖面（状态空间、时间深度、信息敏感性、对手建模、风险、脆弱性）；(3) 锯齿度指标，衡量模型在策略相似游戏间优势的非平滑跳变。从2000个游戏的池中采样50个基准游戏，在9个前沿及开源LLM间进行超过36,000场锦标赛。

Result: 新一代前沿模型平均得分更高。整体强度相近的模型表现出定性不同的能力剖面；前三名中的gpt-5和claude比gemini-3.1-pro在局部上更不稳定（锯齿度更高）。

Conclusion: 能力剖面和锯齿度指标提供了整体排名无法给出的、与部署相关的诊断信息，对于评估LLM在实际策略环境中的行为更具参考价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as economic agents in marketplaces, auctions, and bidding settings. Anticipating their behavior in any specific deployment is hard. Existing strategic-reasoning benchmarks evaluate models on fixed canonical games. These benchmarks may saturate as the frontier improves, and they do not allow evaluators to generalize with confidence from benchmark performance to the varied and messy strategic environments that actual deployments involve. We introduce GENSTRAT, which uses procedurally generated strategic environments to address these challenges. Concretely, we generate a distribution of two-player zero-sum imperfect-information card games. The generator can draw fresh games on demand, allowing for evergreen evaluation and resistance to contamination. We pair the game distribution with a capability-profile methodology that decomposes model competence across six axes (state space, temporal depth, information sensitivity, opponent modeling, risk, and brittleness). We also introduce a jaggedness measure of within-distribution smoothness that detects when a model's advantage jumps unpredictably between strategically similar games. We sample 50 benchmark games from a 2,000-game generated pool and evaluate nine frontier and open-weight LLMs in a head-to-head tournament with over 36,000 matches. Newer frontier-tier models score higher on average. Beyond that average, models with near-identical overall strength show qualitatively different capability profiles, and two of the top three leaderboard models (gpt-5 and claude) are noticeably more locally volatile than the third (gemini-3.1-pro), despite being close in overall strength. Together, the capability profile and the jaggedness measure give a deployment-relevant diagnostic that the overall ranking alone cannot provide.

</details>


### [119] [Ontological Knowledge Blocks: Executable Compliance and Profile-Based Validation for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23297)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Julian M. Kunkel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs)，一种可编程治理基础设施，将监管义务编译为机器可检查的约束，通过在AI辅助HPC资源分配场景中验证，实现了12.6-100.3ms的SHACL验证延迟和严格可加的违规累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI服务合规治理依赖文档化、静态清单和人工审核，无法扩展到自动化AI系统，急需可编程、可自动化验证的治理基础设施。

Method: 将OKB形式化为5元组，绑定规范义务与RDF/OWL概念模式、SHACL验证规则、证据需求和PROV-O溯源链接；设计确定性监管编译器将IR记录转换为可组合的KB模块，实现基于配置文件的治理重配置。

Result: 在24次验证运行和4个治理配置下，SHACL验证延迟12.6-100.3ms，违规严格可加，Combined配置被确认为最全面的配置文件。

Conclusion: OKBs提供了一种可扩展、可编程的自动化合规治理方案，无需修改服务代码即可实现配置重配置，所有工件已开源。

Abstract: AI-enabled services deployed in critical digital infrastructure are subject to governance obligations spanning transparency, accountability, fairness, and traceability. Compliance today remains documentation-centric: obligations are described in prose, audits rely on static checklists, and verification depends on manual review. Such approaches do not scale to automated AI systems. This paper introduces Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs), a programmable governance infrastructure that compiles regulatory obligations into machine-checkable constraints over structured evidence graphs. We formalize an OKB as a 5-tuple that binds normative obligations to an RDF/OWL concept schema, executable SHACL validation rules, explicit evidence requirements, and PROV-O provenance links. A deterministic regulatory compiler translates structured Intermediate Representation (IR) records into composable KB modules, enabling profile-based governance reconfiguration without modifying service code. We implement two prototypes and evaluate them in an AI-assisted HPC resource allocation scenario across 24 validation runs and four governance profiles. Results demonstrate profile-sensitive validation, strictly additive violation accumulation, SHACL validation latency between 12.6 ms and 100.3 ms, and profile equivalence testing confirming Combined as the strictly most comprehensive profile. All artefacts are released as open source.

</details>


### [120] [Human-in-the-Loop Multi-Agent Ventilator Decision Support with Contextual Bandit Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23320)
*Sijia Li,Xiaoyu Tan,Qixing Wang,Weiyi Zhao,Chen Zhan,Teqi Hao,Xuemin Wang,Lei Gu,Roland Eils,Xihe Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种人机协同的多智能体框架VDSS，通过合约驱动的结构化接口协调模块化决策组件，结合上下文赌博机进行在线偏好自适应，并利用结构化拒绝反馈触发目标重规划，在ICU轨迹回放中实现了更高的推荐可接受性和更少的交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有呼吸机决策支持方法中，基于规则的方法难以泛化个性化需求，而端到端强化学习或单一大型语言模型系统难以控制和审计，缺乏可解释性和临床可干预性。

Method: 提出了VDSS框架：1) 人机协同的多智能体架构，通过合约驱动的结构化接口协调模块化决策组件；2) 使用上下文赌博机进行在线偏好自适应，从每次调整周期中最终被接受的决策更新临床医生特定偏好；3) 结构化拒绝反馈触发目标重规划，减少无效迭代并提高交互稳定性。

Result: 回顾性ICU轨迹回放结合专家评估显示，VDSS的推荐可接受性更高，且达到可接受计划所需的交互轮次更少。

Conclusion: VDSS框架支持临床可部署的人机协作，具有良好的控制性、可审计性和个性化适应能力。

Abstract: Ventilator decision support requires sequential decisions that track evolving physiology and disease trajectories while respecting safety boundaries and clinician specific tuning styles. Rule based approaches rarely generalize personalization, and end to end reinforcement learning or single large language model systems remain difficult to control and audit. We propose the Ventilator Decision Support System (VDSS), a human in the loop multi agent framework that coordinates modular decision components through contract driven structured interfaces and produces traceable evidence for review. VDSS performs online preference adaptation with a contextual bandit, updating clinician specific preferences from the final accepted decision at each adjustment cycle and using them to guide subsequent recommendations. Structured rejection feedback triggers targeted replanning to reduce unproductive iterations and improve interaction stability. Retrospective ICU trajectory replay with expert review indicates higher recommendation acceptability and fewer interaction rounds to reach an acceptable plan, supporting clinically deployable human AI collaboration.

</details>


### [121] [When Planning Fails Despite Correct Execution: On Epistemic Calibration for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23414)
*Zehao Wang,Shilong Jin,Zhao Cao,Lanjun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息一致性的校准方法（EPC-AW），用于解决多智能体系统中因智能体对自身知识误判而导致的规划失败问题，实验显示系统级成功率平均提升9.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中，即使动作执行正确，智能体也可能在评估计划可行性时误判自身知识（称为规划中的认知校准偏差），这种偏差在规划阶段是隐性的且动态变化的，现有方法难以捕捉和应对。

Method: 提出EPC-AW框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于信息一致性的计划选择——选择在不同智能体间评估结果稳定的计划；(2) 一致性引导的认知状态精炼——利用过去的计划可行性评估差异来动态调整校准，指导未来规划。

Result: EPC-AW在实验中使系统级成功率平均提升9.75%。

Conclusion: 通过评估计划在不同信息条件下是否仍被支持（而非直接验证可行性），并利用智能体间的一致性信号进行动态校准，可有效缓解规划中的认知校准偏差问题。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems can fail even when planned actions are executed correctly because agents may misjudge their knowledge when evaluating plan feasibility, a phenomenon we term epistemic miscalibration in planning. Unlike execution errors, epistemic miscalibration is latent during planning, as generated plans can remain self-consistent and executable without observable errors; the miscalibration is also dynamic, as new information can alter feasibility assessments, potentially obscuring past miscalibration signals and causing them to recur over time. To address this, we propose the Epistemic Planning Calibration Agentic Workflow (EPC-AW), which assesses whether plans remain supported under varying information conditions rather than directly verifying feasibility. EPC-AW employs Information-consistency-based Plan Selection, selecting plans whose evaluations are stable across agents, together with Consistency-guided Epistemic State Refinement to adapt calibration over time by leveraging past discrepancies to guide future planning. Experiments show that EPC-AW improves system-level success by an average of 9.75%.

</details>


### [122] [EDGE-OPD: Internalizing Privileged Context with Evidence Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23493)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Dylan Bates,Aman Sharma,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中特权信息导致模型行为失控的问题，提出EDGE-OPD方法，通过引导式rollout和证据掩码两种机制，在稀有token/身份设定下实现高效知识迁移，同时保持通用能力不退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD虽能利用特权上下文（如身份、私密事实、解题过程）来提升模型能力，但特权信息会引发非预期的行为改变，如修改推理方式、降低通用能力、影响响应长度和风格等，导致学生模型学到副作用而非目标行为。现有方法（包括带验证器的RLSD）在这种设定下完全失效。

Method: 提出EDGE-OPD方法，包含两个关键设计：a) 引导式rollout：在采样时向学生注入特权上下文行为，确保稀有目标行为出现在在线策略数据中；b) 证据掩码：仅在特权上下文支持当前采样token的位置更新学生模型，而非对rollout中所有token进行更新，从而避免学到无关副作用。

Result: 实验表明，标准OPSD及其变体RLSD（无论是否使用验证器）在目标身份学习任务上完全失败，而EDGE-OPD成功实现了身份学习。掩码区域的消融实验进一步揭示，身份信号集中在正证据尾部区域，为高效知识迁移和通用能力保持提供了有价值的见解。

Conclusion: EDGE-OPD通过引导式rollout和证据掩码有效解决了OPSD中特权信息导致的副作用问题，实现了对稀有目标行为的高效、精准迁移，同时保留了模型的通用能力。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has gained wide attraction as an LLM post-training paradigm due to its effectiveness in improving capabilities without introducing model distribution drift, and consequently, regression in general tasks. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) is an efficient use-case of OPD, which is appealing as it requires only a single model as a student and teacher, and it also has the benefit of providing privileged context that is a absent at inference time (e.g. a persona, a private fact, or a worked solution) to the teacher during the training process. The challenge in this approach is that the privileged information can change model behavior more than intended: it can modify reasoning, degrade general capabilities, and affect performance indicators like response length, style, or local token preferences. Consequently, OPSD may train the student on side effects rather than a desired, transferable behavior. In this paper, we study this problem in a rare-token/identity setting and propose EviDence GuidEd On-Policy Distillation (EDGE-OPD), a modification of OPSD with two distinct characteristics: a) it uses guided rollouts to inject privileged-context behavior to the student at sampling time, so that the rare target behavior is actually present in the on-policy data, and b) it applies an evidence mask: the student is updated only at token positions where the privileged context supports the sampled token, rather than on every token in the rollout. We empirically show that OPSD (and its variant RLSD, with and without a verifier) completely fail to learn a target identity, while the integration of guided rollouts allows them to succeed. Additionally, mask-region ablations show that the persona signal is localized to the positive-evidence tail, allows us to draw valuable insights about efficient knowledge transfer and preservation of general purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [123] [CP or DP? Why Not Both: A Case Study in the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23569)
*Emma Legrand,Roger Kameugne,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将动态规划（DP）和约束规划（CP）混合的方法，用于解决部分车间调度问题（PSSP），其中DP作为主搜索框架，CP作为子程序利用全局约束传播，并支持任意时间DP策略和大邻域搜索（LNS）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP和CP通常是分开使用的两种求解组合优化问题的范式，本文旨在展示两者可以有效且优雅地结合，以克服各自单独使用时的局限性。

Method: 以DP为主搜索框架，CP作为子程序来利用全局约束传播；将这种方法应用于部分车间调度问题（PSSP）；支持任意时间DP策略（如任意时间列搜索），而原有DP算法仅以严格分层方式运行；利用CP建模的灵活性自然地处理任意优先约束；设计了大邻域搜索（LNS）方案，在重启时重用DP模型并施加偏序调度以改进当前最优解。

Result: 该方法虽然在与针对该问题的专用纯CP求解器的竞争中不占优势，但成功证明了DP与CP混合集成的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于展示了DP与CP混合集成的可行性，为后续将两种范式结合求解组合优化问题提供了新的思路和框架。

Abstract: Dynamic Programming (DP) and Constraint Programming (CP) are well-established paradigms for solving combinatorial optimization problems. Usually, these two approaches are used separately. This paper aims to show that the two can be combined effectively and elegantly, with DP serving as the primary search framework and CP used as a subroutine to leverage global constraint propagation. This paper presents such an approach for the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem (PSSP), for which a pure DP method has previously been proposed, and efficient CP filtering algorithms are available. The PSSP is a general scheduling problem where each job consists of a set of operations with arbitrary precedence constraints. The approach is flexible enough to accommodate anytime DP strategies, such as anytime column search, whereas the original DP algorithm operated in a strictly layer-wise manner. Moreover, the flexibility of the CP modeling makes it straightforward to incorporate arbitrary precedence constraints. As a result, the model naturally handles any precedence graph and even enables the design of a Large Neighborhood Search (LNS) scheme, in which the DP model is reused, and partial-order schedules are imposed across restarts to improve the incumbent solution. While not competitive with state-of-the-art pure CP solvers for this specific problem, our primary contribution is demonstrating the viability of this hybrid integration.

</details>


### [124] [Co-ReAct: Rubrics as Step-Level Collaborators for ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23590)
*Jiazheng Kang,Bowen Zhang,Zixin Song,Jiangwang Chen,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Co-ReAct 提出一种基于规则（rubric）的推理时动作选择框架，通过在每一步注入规则来引导搜索型智能体的思考与行动决策，并训练专用的规则生成器（使用GRPO优化Spearman排序奖励），显著提升了深度搜索任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReAct风格的智能体在多步推理任务中依赖内部判断来决策搜索、推理和停止，容易产生浅层、冗余或目标不明确的轨迹。以往将规则作为外部质量信号的工作主要用于训练奖励或事后评估，缺乏步骤级的实时指导能力。

Method: 提出Co-ReAct框架：1) 在推理的每个决策步骤，将规则注入智能体的上下文，指导下一步的推理或行动选择；2) 使用GRPO训练专用的规则生成器，优化目标是多专家共识排名的列表级Spearman秩相关奖励，而非传统的成对或二元偏好。

Result: 在DeepResearchBench和SQA-CS-V2上，Co-ReAct在基于8B/14B开源模型和闭源前沿模型的搜索智能体上，一致优于ReAct及其他代表性测试时计算基线。训练的规则生成器可作为即插即用组件提升基线性能。

Conclusion: 步骤级规则引导能有效改善深度搜索任务中智能体的推理质量，列表级排序优化比成对偏好训练出更具判别力的规则生成器。

Abstract: ReAct-style agents for search-intensive, multi-step reasoning tasks rely largely on their own internal judgment to decide what evidence to seek, which reasoning or action step to take next, and when to stop, often producing shallow, redundant, or poorly targeted trajectories. Prior work has explored rubrics as external quality signals, but existing uses are mostly evaluative rather than action-guiding: rubrics typically serve as training-time rewards or post-hoc evaluators of completed outputs, and in deep-research settings they are often coarse-grained and report-level rather than step-level. We introduce Co-ReAct, a rubric-guided action-selection framework that uses rubrics as step-level guidance during inference. At each decision step, Co-ReAct injects a rubric into the agent's context to guide the next Reason-or-Act decision, specifying what the agent should target in evidence seeking, search, reasoning, or self-evaluation. To make this guidance reliable, we train a dedicated rubric generator with GRPO. Unlike prior pairwise or binary preference formulations, our objective optimizes a list-wise Spearman rank-correlation reward against multi-judge expert consensus rankings, encouraging rubrics that are discriminative rather than merely plausible. On DeepResearchBench and SQA-CS-V2, Co-ReAct consistently improves over ReAct and representative test-time compute baselines across search agents built on both 8B/14B open-source and frontier closed-source base models. The trained rubric generator can also serve as a drop-in component that improves these baselines without changing their underlying decision mechanisms. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZBWpro/Co-ReAct.

</details>


### [125] [One Policy, Infinite NPCs: Persona-Traceable Shared RL Policies for Scalable Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23652)
*Yoosung Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PCSP（Persona Conditioned Shared Policy）方法，通过单一强化学习策略结合冻结的LLM个性嵌入，实现可扩展、实时、个性一致的非玩家角色控制，在300人设基准上达到随机水平17倍的零样本个性识别，Spearman ρ≈0.73语义-行为对齐，推理速度比LLM策略基线快22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在生活模拟游戏中缺乏同时满足个性一致性、可控性和实时推理能力的NPC控制方案，需要一种可扩展的个性条件策略。

Method: PCSP包含：每个NPC一次性个性编码、低秩个性投影、神经个性条件调制、PPO+InfoNCE一致性+KL散度多样性联合训练目标。

Result: 零样本个性识别达随机水平17倍；Spearman ρ≈0.73语义-行为对齐；推理速度比LLM策略基线快22倍；InfoNCE损失是关键组件；在Melting Pot 2.4.0和UE5引擎中得到验证。

Conclusion: 共享RL策略可以支持可扩展、实时、个性条件的NPC控制，证明了该方法在商业游戏引擎中的部署可行性。

Abstract: On a 300-persona life-simulation benchmark, pcsp achieves compositional zero-shot persona identification up to 17x above chance, Spearman rho approx 0.73 semantic-behavioral alignment, and 22x faster inference than an LLM-as-policy baseline. Life simulation games require hundreds to thousands of non-player characters (NPCs) that behave consistently with distinct personalities while remaining controllable through designer-authored natural language. Existing methods fail on constraints like persona consistency, controllability, or real-time inference. We introduce pcsp (Persona Conditioned Shared Policy), a single reinforcement learning policy conditioned on frozen LLM embeddings of free-form persona descriptions. pcsp combines once-per-NPC persona encoding, low-rank persona projection, neural persona conditioning, and a PPO + InfoNCE consistency + KL diversity training objective. Across three experimental settings, ablations show that the InfoNCE trajectory-consistency objective is load bearing: removing it collapses zero-shot persona identification to chance. External validation on Melting Pot 2.4.0 substrates confirms that our method produces persona-conditioned behavioral divergence in multi-agent strategic environments. We distinguish two senses of held-out evaluation: compositional zero-shot and vocabulary-expansion held-out. Finally, a UE5 deployment reproduces the in-engine persona-conditioning ablation at 64 agents with a low failure rate, showing that the sub-frame inference profile survives in a commercial game engine. These results prove that shared RL policies can support scalable, real-time, persona-conditioned NPC control.

</details>


### [126] [MemAudit: Post-hoc Auditing of Poisoned Agent Memory via Causal Attribution and Structural Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23723)
*Zhewen Tan,Yilun Yao,Huiyan Jin,Wenhan Yu,Guoan Wang,Mengyuan Fan,liang lu,Feng Liu,Xiangzheng Zhang,Duohe Ma,Tong Yang,Lin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemAudit，一种用于记忆增强型LLM智能体的事后因果记忆审计框架。该框架结合反事实记忆影响分数和记忆一致性图两种互补信号，有效识别导致有害输出的恶意记忆。在QA和推理智能体场景下，将攻击成功率从70%-83.3%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要关注在线干预（如提示过滤或输出阻断），但无法解决有害行为已发生后，如何追溯哪些存储记忆应被问责的事后归因问题。

Method: MemAudit包含两个互补信号：(1) 反事实记忆影响分数，衡量每条记忆对有害输出的因果贡献；(2) 记忆一致性图，识别记忆库中结构异常的记忆。

Result: 在QA场景下，攻击成功率从70%降至0%；在RAP推理智能体场景下，攻击成功率从83.3%降至0%。

Conclusion: MemAudit在真实事后审计场景中能显著降低记忆注入攻击的成功率，有效弥补了现有在线防御机制的不足。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on persistent memory to store past interactions, retrieve relevant demonstrations, and improve long-horizon task execution. However, this memory mechanism also creates a practical security vulnerability: an adversarial user may inject malicious records into the agent's memory through ordinary interaction, and these records can later be retrieved to steer the agent's reasoning and actions. Existing defenses primarily focus on online intervention, such as prompt filtering or output blocking, but they do not address the post-hoc question of which stored memories are responsible after harmful behavior has already been observed. We propose \textbf{MemAudit}, a post-hoc causal memory auditing framework for memory-augmented LLM agents. The framework combines two complementary signals: (1) a counterfactual memory influence score that measures each memory's causal contribution to harmful outputs, and (2) a memory consistency graph that identifies structurally anomalous memories within the broader memory store. We evaluate MemAudit against MINJA, a query-only memory injection attack in which malicious records are generated and stored through normal agent interactions rather than direct memory-bank modification. Across both QA and reasoning-agent settings, MemAudit substantially reduces attack success rates under realistic post-hoc auditing scenarios. The results show that QA attack success is reduced from $70\%$ to $0\%$, while RAP attack success drops from $83.3\%$ to $0\%$.

</details>


### [127] [Agentic Proving for Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23772)
*Alessandro Sosso,Akhil Arora,Bas Spitters*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了Claude Code在Lean 4基准测试CLEVER上的智能体证明框架表现，发现其在规格生成、实现验证和端到端流水线上均达到极高成功率（98.8%、87.5%、98.1%），表明现有程序验证基准已难以匹配现代智能体证明器的能力，亟需更严谨、抗bug的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究智能体系统在自动定理证明方面的能力是否能够拓展到程序验证领域，评估当前最先进的智能体证明框架在形式化程序验证上的实际表现。

Method: 在CLEVER（Lean 4可验证代码生成基准）上使用Claude Code构建智能体证明框架，评估其在规格生成、实现验证和端到端程序生成与验证流水线三个阶段的性能，并通过人工审查确认其反馈质量。

Result: Claude在98.8%的问题上生成了可接受的规范（其中81.3%通过了CLEVER的基于同构的评分）；对正确的ground-truth规范，87.5%的实现得到认证；在前提自洽的条目上，端到端流水线成功率达98.1%。所有阶段中Claude均能提供高质量的自我反馈。

Conclusion: 现有程序验证基准的难度已落后于现代智能体证明器的能力，需要更严格、抗bug的评估方法（尤其是替代基于同构的规范评分）；编译器紧密集成的智能体范式是当前基础程序验证最有效的方法。

Abstract: Agentic systems have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for automated theorem proving in formal mathematics. To assess how far these capabilities extend to program verification, we evaluate Claude Code in an agentic proving framework on CLEVER, a Lean 4 benchmark for verifiable code generation. Our results show that Claude generates arguably valid specifications for 98.8% of problems (with 81.3% also accepted by CLEVER's isomorphism-based scoring on the correct portion of the benchmark), certifies implementations against correct ground-truth specifications for 87.5% of problems, and reaches a 98.1% success rate on the end-to-end program generation and verification pipeline over entries with self-consistent premises. Across all stages, Claude further provides high-quality feedback on its own attempts (as confirmed under manual review), identifying underlying causes of failure and lingering bugs in the dataset. These findings highlight a growing mismatch between the difficulty of existing program verification benchmarks and the capabilities of modern agentic provers, and point to the need for more rigorous, bug-resilient evaluation methodologies, and in particular for alternatives to isomorphism-based scoring of generated specifications. More broadly, our results provide empirical evidence that tight compiler-in-the-loop agentic paradigms are currently the most effective approach for foundational program verification.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Binary Edits Robust Multimodal Knowledge Editing with Adversarial Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23780)
*Haoyuan Wang,Xiaohao Liu,Jiajie Su,Jianmao Xiao,Chaochao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASAM方法，通过潜在对抗鲁棒化（LAR）和秩约束子空间学习（RCSL）来增强多模态大语言模型知识编辑的泛化性，解决传统方法在语义等价变体上编辑传播不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型需要高效的知识更新机制，但现有内在多模态知识编辑方法在泛化性上表现有限，无法将编辑传播到语义等价的视觉和语言变体上，原因是缺乏显式语义监督、编辑范围僵化以及在高维多模态空间中过度锚定于单个样本。

Method: 提出ASAM框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 潜在对抗鲁棒化（LAR），在联合潜在空间中生成对抗但语义一致的变体以暴露脆弱语义区域；2) 秩约束子空间学习（RCSL），通过基于奇异值的目标函数在编辑层强制实现对抗表示的低秩对齐。

Result: 大量实验分析证明了ASAM方法在增强多模态知识编辑泛化性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式针对泛化性进行鲁棒的内在多模态知识编辑设计，ASAM有效解决了传统方法在语义等价变体上编辑传播不足的问题。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) need efficient mechanisms to update knowledge without degrading existing capabilities. While intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing achieves strong reliability and locality, it often exhibits limited generality, failing to propagate edits across semantically equivalent visual and linguistic variations. This issue arises from the lack of explicit semantic supervision, rigid editing scopes, and biased anchoring to individual samples in high-dimensional multimodal spaces. We address robust intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing by explicitly targeting generalization. We formalize robustness through knowledge units that group semantically equivalent multimodal inputs and define generality as consistent predictions within each unit. To expose fragile semantic regions, we introduce Latent Adversarial Robustification (LAR), which generates adversarial yet semantically coherent variants in the joint latent space. We further propose Rank-Constrained Subspace Learning (RCSL), enforcing low-rank alignment of adversarial representations at the edit layer via a singular value-based objective. Extensive analysis demonstrates the effectiveness of ASAM empirically.

</details>


### [129] [SPACENUM: Revisiting Spatial Numerical Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23898)
*Jianshu Zhang,Yijiang Li,Huifeixin Chen,Haoran Lu,Letian Xue,Bingyang Wang,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaceNum框架，系统评估视觉-语言模型（VLM）在具身环境中对空间数值（如动作幅度、空间坐标）的理解能力。通过Num2Space和Space2Num两个双向任务，涵盖动态探索与静态布局两种场景，发现现有VLM在空间数值理解上表现接近随机猜测，依赖浅层空间线索，缺乏稳定的坐标感知表征和抽象空间结构能力。微调可部分改善性能，但显式推理收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管VLM在具身环境中被广泛部署并输出看似有意义的数值（如动作幅度、空间坐标），但这些数值输出是否真正基于空间感知尚不清楚。因此，本文旨在系统性地重新审视VLM的空间数值理解能力。

Method: 提出SpaceNum统一框架，包含两种互补设置：动态探索中的数值变化（动态过渡）和空间推理中的静态布局。设计两个双向任务——Num2Space（从数值映射到空间结构）和Space2Num（从空间结构映射到数值）。通过误差分析、推理轨迹分析和受控干预实验，系统评估当前VLM的表现。

Result: 模型在空间数值理解任务上表现接近随机猜测，严重依赖浅层空间线索，难以构建稳定的坐标感知表征，无法从视觉观察中抽象出结构化空间布局。显式推理仅带来边际提升，而微调可部分改善空间数值理解能力，并迁移到外部空间推理基准。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在空间数值理解方面存在根本性缺陷，数值输出并非真正基于空间感知。该问题通过简单微调可得到部分缓解，但显式推理的增益有限，表明需要更根本的改进方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in embodied environments, where they need produce numerical outputs such as action magnitudes and spatial coordinates. Although these numbers appear meaningful, it remains unclear whether these numerical outputs are genuinely grounded in spatial perception. Therefore, in this work, we revisit spatial numerical understanding through SpaceNum, a unified framework that captures two complementary settings: numbers as dynamic transitions during spatial exploration, and numbers as static layouts in spatial reasoning. We formulate two bidirectional tasks, Num2Space and Space2Num, to evaluate how well VLMs map between vision-side spatial structure and language-side numerical representations. We systematically study whether current VLMs truly understand numerical values in spatial settings. Across dynamic transitions and static layouts, we find that models largely fail to ground numbers in spatial meaning and often perform close to random guess. Through error analysis, reasoning trace analysis, and controlled interventions, we show that current VLMs rely heavily on shallow spatial cues, struggle to build stable coordinate-aware representations, and fail to abstract structured spatial layouts from visual observations. We further show that explicit reasoning provides only marginal gains, while tuning can partially improve spatial numerical understanding and transfer to external spatial reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [130] [From Raw Experience to Skill Consumption: A Systematic Study of Model-Generated Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23899)
*Zisu Huang,Jingwen Xu,Yifan Yang,Ziyang Gong,Qihao Yang,Muzhao Tian,Xiaohua Wang,Changze Lv,Xuemei Gao,Qi Dai,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Xue Yang,Dongdong Chen,Xiaoqing Zheng,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了技能全生命周期（经验生成→技能提取→技能消费）的效用评估框架，发现模型生成的技能平均有益但存在负迁移，提取器和消费者的能力并不对称，并据此提出元技能来提升技能质量、减少负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管领域级和模型生成的技能在语言智能体中日益流行且提取方法不断涌现，但缺乏对技能完整生命周期（经验生成、技能提取、技能消费）的系统性研究，不清楚这些技能是否真正有效、何时有效以及成功或失败的原因。

Method: 构建基于效用的评估框架，在五个多样化的智能体任务领域上进行系统实验，涵盖多种提取器和目标智能体。然后深入分析生命周期各阶段，研究经验组成如何塑造技能质量、有用技能的特征属性以及同一技能在不同消费者间的迁移表现。最后将发现转化为具体的元技能指导技能提取。

Result: 模型生成的技能平均有益但存在不可忽视的负迁移；提取器与消费者的能力不对称（强提取器可能是弱消费者）；技能效用与模型规模或基线任务强度无关；提出的元技能一致性地提升技能质量并显著减少负迁移。

Conclusion: 技能效用受生命周期各阶段复杂因素共同影响，元技能可作为有效的干预手段提升技能提取的实际效用。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly improve by reusing \emph{skills} -- structured procedural artifacts distilled from past experience. In particular, \emph{domain-level} and \emph{model-generated} skills are especially promising. They offer fast adaptation within a domain by encoding domain-specific recurring procedures, and they scale beyond labor-intensive hand-crafting. However, while extraction methods continue to proliferate, understanding remains limited, with no comprehensive study spanning the full skill lifecycle -- \textbf{experience generation}, \textbf{skill extraction}, and \textbf{skill consumption} -- to ask whether such skills actually work, when they work, and what makes them succeed or fail. To close this gap, we build a utility-grounded evaluation framework that provides systematic experimental results across extractors and target agents, covering five diverse agentic task domains. We find that model-generated skills are beneficial on average but exhibit non-trivial negative transfer, and that neither extractors nor targets behave uniformly. A model can be a strong extractor yet a weak consumer, or vice versa, with skill utility independent of model scale or baseline task strength. To explain these patterns, we then dissect each lifecycle stage in depth, analyzing how experience composition shapes skill quality, what properties characterize useful skills, and how the same skill transfers across different consumers. Finally, we translate these findings into a concrete \emph{meta-skill} that guides skill extraction toward the features tied to actual utility, which consistently improves skill quality across domains and substantially reduces negative transfer.

</details>


### [131] [SkillOpt: Executive Strategy for Self-Evolving Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23904)
*Yifan Yang,Ziyang Gong,Weiquan Huang,Qihao Yang,Ziwei Zhou,Zisu Huang,Yan Li,Xuemei Gao,Qi Dai,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Yuqing Yang,Dongdong Chen,Xue Yang,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillOpt是首个系统性的、可控的文本空间技能优化器，通过类似深度学习的训练范式（验证集评估、学习率预算、拒绝编辑缓冲、epoch级慢更新）对智能体技能文档进行增/删/改编辑，严格保证每次编辑都提升验证得分。在6个基准、7个目标模型、3种执行框架下，52个评估单元全部达到最佳或并列最佳，相比无技能基线在GPT-5.5上提升+19.1~+24.8个准确率点，且优化后的技能可跨模型、跨执行环境迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能生成方式（手工构建、一次性生成、松散自修正）缺乏类似深度学习优化器的严格训练范式，无法在反馈下可靠地持续改进技能质量。

Method: 将技能视为冻结智能体的外部状态，用一个独立的优化器模型将带分数的轨迹转化为对技能文档的有界增/删/改编辑，仅当编辑在验证集上严格提升得分时接受。引入文本学习率预算、拒绝编辑缓冲区和epoch级慢/元更新机制，训练过程稳定且部署时零额外推理开销。

Result: 在6个基准、7个目标模型、3种执行框架共52个评估单元上全部取得最佳或并列最佳。在GPT-5.5上，直接聊天提升+23.5，Codex智能体循环提升+24.8，Claude Code提升+19.1个准确率点。优化后的技能可跨模型规模、跨执行环境（Codex与Claude Code之间）以及跨相近数学基准迁移。

Conclusion: SkillOpt验证了将技能训练作为冻结智能体外部状态、采用深度学习式优化纪律的可行性，为智能体技能的可控、可复现、可迁移优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Agent skills today are hand-crafted, generated one-shot, or evolved through loosely controlled self-revision, none of which behaves like a deep-learning optimizer for the skill, and none of which reliably improves over its starting point under feedback. We argue the skill should instead be trained as the external state of a frozen agent, with the same discipline that makes weight-space optimization reproducible. SkillOpt is, to our knowledge, the first systematic controllable text-space optimizer for agent skills: a separate optimizer model turns scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits on a single skill document, and an edit is accepted only when it strictly improves a held-out validation score. A textual learning-rate budget, rejected-edit buffer, and epoch-wise slow/meta update make skill training stable while adding zero inference-time model calls at deployment. Across six benchmarks, seven target models, and three execution harnesses (direct chat, Codex, Claude Code), SkillOpt is best or tied on all 52 evaluated (model, benchmark, harness) cells and beats every per-cell competitor among human, one-shot LLM, Trace2Skill, TextGrad, GEPA, and EvoSkill skills. On GPT-5.5 it lifts the average no-skill accuracy by +23.5 points in direct chat, by +24.8 inside the Codex agentic loop, and by +19.1 inside Claude Code. Transfer experiments further show that optimized skill artifacts retain value when moved across model scales, between Codex and Claude Code execution environments, and to a nearby math benchmark without further optimization.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Extending Deep Event Visual Odometry with Sparse Point-Cloud Export](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22890)
*Alireza Safdari,Sajad Ashraf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在DEVO单目事件相机视觉里程计基础上，扩展了一个稀疏点云导出管线，将内部估计的3D结构转换为显式点云表示，用于可视化和后续处理，并在BOARD SLOW序列上验证了局部一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DEVO模型虽在事件相机视觉里程计上表现优异，但其内部估计的3D结构缺乏显式点云导出能力，限制了可视化与后续几何处理。

Method: 不修改核心里程计公式，而是暴露DEVO内部已估计的3D结构，将其转换为显式点云表示；同时实现数据导出、格式转换和点云清理的工作流。

Result: 在BOARD SLOW序列上，导出稀疏点云与EMVS重建在5cm阈值下达到高精度局部一致性，但存在密度低、完整性不足和对累积里程计噪声敏感的局限。

Conclusion: 该工作在不改动原始视觉里程计管线的前提下，成功实现了稀疏几何场景输出，验证了可行性并指出了稀疏点云导出的固有局限。

Abstract: Event cameras are well suited for visual odometry under high-speed motion and challenging lighting conditions due to their low latency, high temporal resolution, and high dynamic range. Deep Event Visual Odometry (DEVO) demonstrated that monocular event-only odometry can achieve strong performance by combining sparse patch tracking, learned patch selection, recurrent correspondence refinement, and differentiable bundle adjustment. In this project, we extend DEVO with a sparse point-cloud export pipeline. Rather than modifying the core odometry formulation, our approach exposes the internal 3D structure already estimated by DEVO and converts it into an explicit point-cloud representation for visualization and further processing. In addition, we implement a practical workflow for data export, format conversion, and point-cloud cleanup. The resulting system preserves the original visual odometry pipeline while enabling sparse geometric scene output. Experiments on the BOARD SLOW sequence show that the exported sparse cloud is locally consistent with EMVS reconstructions, achieving high precision at a 5 cm threshold, while also highlighting the expected limitations in density, completeness, and sensitivity to accumulated odometry noise.

</details>


### [133] [Agentic-VLA: Efficient Online Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22896)
*Ruofan Jin,Zaixi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Agentic-VLA 是一种智能训练框架，通过自适应奖励合成、语言引导探索和经验记忆三大创新，使 VLA 模型能够在线高效适应新环境，在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin 2.0 基准上显著提升了泛化能力、样本效率和学习速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLA 训练方法存在两个关键局限：对新颖环境的泛化能力差，以及需要大量演示数据导致训练效率低下。因此需要一种能够在线高效适应的训练框架。

Method: 提出 Agentic-VLA 框架，包含三项创新：(1) 自适应奖励合成：根据 VLA 当前能力和任务复杂度动态生成调整奖励函数，将复杂任务分解为可学习的子目标进行课程学习；(2) 语言引导探索：利用评论模型提供结构化指导进行系统探索，替代随机采样；(3) 经验记忆：存储并检索任务相关的策略权重，用于相似任务的暖启动适应。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上：长时域任务 +12.3%，单样本学习 +28.5%，跨任务迁移从 0% 提升至 31.2%；收敛速度比现有在线适应方法快 2.4 倍。在 RoboTwin 2.0 双机械臂基准（含随机Hard设置）上同样保持优势。

Conclusion: Agentic-VLA 是迈向真正自适应、可在部署中持续学习的 VLA 系统的重要一步。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by leveraging pre-trained vision-language representations. However, current VLA training methods suffer from two critical limitations: poor generalization to novel environments and low training efficiency requiring extensive demonstrations. We introduce Agentic-VLA, an agentic training framework that enables VLAs to efficiently adapt online through three key innovations: (1) Adaptive Reward Synthesis, which dynamically generates and adjusts reward functions based on the VLA's current capabilities and task complexity, decomposing complex tasks into learnable sub-goals for curriculum learning; (2) Language-Guided Exploration, where a critic model provides structured guidance for systematic exploration rather than random sampling; and (3) Experience Memory,which stores and retrieves task-relevant policy weights for warm-starting adaptation to similar tasks. We evaluate Agentic-VLA on the LIBERO benchmark, achieving substantial improvements: +12.3% on long-horizon tasks, +28.5% in 1-shot learning, and enabling cross-task transfer from 0% to 31.2% without task-specific demonstrations. Our framework also demonstrates 2.4x faster convergence compared to existing online adaptation methods. Beyond LIBERO, Agentic-VLA retains its advantage on the dual-arm RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, including under its randomized Hard setting. These results establish Agentic-VLA as a significant step toward truly adaptive VLA systems capable of continuous learning in deployment.

</details>


### [134] [Robots That Know What to Ask: Recovering Misaligned Rewards through Targeted Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22986)
*Helena Merker,Nick Walker,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从非完美示范中检测未充分规范特征并主动引导用户提供针对性修正示范的框架，通过分析示范中特征的方差来识别不确定性，并利用自然语言解释引导用户补充缺失信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从示范中学习奖励函数时，示范往往是不完美的：人类可能因认知负荷或物理难度而忽视某些特征，或训练数据未能覆盖所有相关场景，导致重要特征未被充分规范，造成奖励函数学习中的歧义和部署时的行为偏差。

Method: 1) 利用特征在不同示范间的方差作为统计信号：一致优化的特征方差小，未充分规范的特征方差大。2) 机器人识别出不确定的特征后，用自然语言解释哪些特征不明确，并主动查询要求提供针对性的修正示范。3) 在模拟桌面操作域和真实Franka机器人用户研究中验证。

Result: 相比随机查询和被动数据收集，基于解释的目标导向查询显著提升了奖励函数恢复效果，减少了在非完美示范学习中本会持续存在的歧义。

Conclusion: 通过主动检测和针对性查询未充分规范的特征，可以有效弥补非完美示范的不足，提高从示范中学习奖励函数的准确性和对齐性。

Abstract: Learning reward functions from demonstrations assumes that demonstrations provide adequate supervision over all features -- or task-relevant aspects of behavior. In practice, demonstrations are often imperfect: humans may under-emphasize certain features due to cognitive load or physical difficulty, or the training regime may fail to sufficiently cover all relevant situations. In either case, important features may be underspecified, leading to ambiguity in the learned reward function and misaligned behavior at deployment. We propose a framework that detects such underspecified features and actively solicits targeted corrective demonstrations. Our key insight is that demonstrations implicitly reveal which features are well specified: features that are consistently optimized show little variation across demonstrations, while features that are underspecified vary widely. We leverage this statistical signal to infer which features may have been insufficiently demonstrated. The robot then explains which features it is uncertain about in natural language and queries for demonstrations that explicitly address the identified gaps. We evaluate our approach in a simulated tabletop manipulation domain and in a user study with a real Franka robot. Targeted, explanation-guided queries significantly improve reward recovery compared to random querying and passive data collection, reducing ambiguity that would otherwise persist in learning from imperfect demonstrations.

</details>


### [135] [PIMbot: A Self-Adaptive Attack Framework for Adversarial Manipulation of Multi-Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23027)
*Zexin Li,Ziliang Zhang,Hyoseung Kim,Cong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PIMbot框架，通过奖励通道的激励操纵和智能体自身策略操纵两种互补杠杆，结合自适应多目标控制器在线平衡，在多机器人社会困境中实现结果操纵。实验在Gazebo仿真和NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano真实嵌入式平台上验证了有效性，该框架可作为暴露多机器人协作任务中关键漏洞的压力测试工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人协作中，社会困境下机器人面临自身利益与集体利益的权衡，而环境因素（如错误通信和对抗性机器人）会影响协作。因此，探索如何操纵多机器人通信以达成不同结果至关重要。

Method: 提出PIMbot框架，包含两个互补杠杆：(i)奖励通道的激励操纵和(ii)智能体自身策略操纵，并采用自适应多目标控制器在线平衡这两种杠杆。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真多机器人环境以及NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano真实嵌入式系统上验证了PIMbot的有效性，量化了系统成本。

Conclusion: PIMbot可作为严格的压力测试工具，暴露多机器人协作任务中的关键漏洞。

Abstract: Recent research has demonstrated the potential of reinforcement learning in effective multi-robot collaboration, particularly in social dilemmas where robots face a trade-off between self-interest and collective benefits. However, environmental factors such as miscommunication and adversarial robots can impact cooperation, making it crucial to explore how multi-robot communication can be manipulated to achieve different outcomes. This paper presents PIMbot, a framework that manipulates outcomes via two complementary levers: (i) incentive manipulation of the reward channel and (ii) policy manipulation of an agent's own actions. An adaptive multi-objective controller balances these levers in an online manner. Our work introduces a novel approach to manipulation in recent multi-agent RL social dilemmas that utilize a unique reward function for incentivization. By utilizing our proposed PIMbot mechanisms, a robot is able to manipulate the social dilemma environment effectively. Comprehensive experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed methods in the Gazebo-simulated multi-robot environment. Moreover, a real embedded device case study on NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano quantifies system cost and validates PIMbot's effectiveness on realistic autonomous embedded systems scenarios beyond simulation. Together, these results position PIMbot as a rigorous stress-test tool exposing critical vulnerabilities in multi-robot cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [136] [Droneulator: A Portable UAV Simulator for Agricultural Workflows with RotorPy and Godot 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23386)
*Jacob Swindell,Michael Lowen,Marija Popovic,Riccardo Polvara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Droneulator是一个结合RotorPy动力学与Godot 4渲染的便携式农业无人机仿真架构，支持PX4控制、WebSocket指令和Zenoh/ROS2管线，可在不修改基础设施的前提下同时支持3D重建数据采集、局部规划和强化学习实验，验证了其在农业巡检中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业无人机仿真需要集成真实3D场景、高保真车辆动力学和机器人中间件，同时保持在不同异构开发机器上的可部署性，现有方案难以同时满足这些需求。

Method: 提出Droneulator架构：1）结合RotorPy多旋翼动力学与Godot 4渲染和传感器生成；2）暴露PX4控制和轻量级WebSocket指令路径；3）通过Zenoh构建ROS2兼容管线发布同步视觉与状态流。在三个农业工作流中验证：COLMAP树尺度3D重建、EGO-Planner冠层障碍物局部规划、基于Gymnasium的闭环强化学习。

Result: 仿真器能维持低延迟感知、支持变密度采集下的重建数据收集、执行无碰撞冠层局部规划、并支持基于深度感知的障碍物避让策略训练。

Conclusion: Droneulator在单一可部署栈中同时支持农业无人机的巡检、规划和学习任务，展示了其在农业应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Agricultural UAV research requires simulators that integrate realistic 3D scenes, high-fidelity vehicle dynamics, and robotics middleware, while remaining practical to deploy across heterogeneous development machines. We present Droneulator, a portable UAV simulator architecture that combines RotorPy for multirotor dynamics with Godot 4 for rendering and sensor generation. Droneulator exposes both PX4-based control and a lightweight WebSocket command path, and publishes synchronised visual and state streams through a Zenoh-based ROS~2-compatible pipeline. This integration enables a single stack to support inspection-oriented data capture, ROS~2/PX4 local planning, and reinforcement learning experiments without modifying the simulator infrastructure. We present quantified validation of the current system across three agricultural UAV workflows: tree-scale image collection for 3D reconstruction with COLMAP, local planning around canopy obstacles using EGO-Planner, and closed-loop reinforcement learning through a custom Gymnasium environment. In the reported setup, the results show that the simulator can sustain low-latency sensing, support reconstruction-oriented data collection under varying capture density, execute collision-free local planning around canopy obstacles, and support stable depth-sensing-based policy training for obstacle-aware navigation. Together, these results show the potential of Droneulator for agricultural UAV inspection, planning, and learning within one deployable stack.

</details>


### [137] [Direct Dynamic Retargeting for Humanoid Imitation Learning from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23762)
*Constant Roux,Ludovic De Matteïs,Armand Jordana,Valentin Guillet,Nicolas Mansard,Olivier Stasse,Philippe Souères*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出直接动态重定向（DDR），一种从单目视频直接生成人形机器人动态可行轨迹的单阶段框架，通过绕过几何偏置，在任务空间中利用基于采样的MPC求解器优化复杂接触序列，在示教跟踪精度、RL训练收敛速度和最终行为执行上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法通过几何重定向或间接动态重定向将人类运动映射到人形机器人，但这些中间运动学投影引入了几何偏置，限制了搜索空间并导致次优的动态行为。

Method: 提出直接动态重定向（DDR），这是一个单阶段框架，在任务空间中将问题形式化，并利用物理仿真器中基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）求解器，直接从专家视频生成高保真、动态可行的轨迹，同时优化复杂的接触序列并减轻输入漂移。

Result: 实验表明，绕过几何偏置使得DDR在示教跟踪精度上优于现有最先进基线方法；此外，为RL智能体提供这种物理可行的参考轨迹加速了训练收敛，并增强了敏捷和平衡行为的最终执行效果。

Conclusion: 直接动态重定向（DDR）有效地解决了人形机器人从人类视频中模仿学习时的形态不匹配问题，为从示教到强化学习训练提供了一条更高效、更高质量的途径。

Abstract: Imitation Learning from monocular video demonstrations provides a scalable approach for teaching complex skills to humanoid robots. However, translating human motion to humanoids requires overcoming significant morphological mismatches. Standard approaches rely on Geometric Retargeting or Indirect Dynamic Retargeting pipelines. We identify that these intermediate kinematic projections introduce a geometric bias, restricting the search space and yielding suboptimal dynamic behaviors. In this paper, we propose Direct Dynamic Retargeting (DDR), a novel single-stage framework that generates high-fidelity, dynamically feasible trajectories directly from expert videos. By formulating the problem in the task space and leveraging a sampling-based Model Predictive Control solver within a physics simulator, DDR natively optimizes over complex contact sequences while mitigating input drift. Our experiments demonstrate that bypassing the geometric bias allows DDR to outperform state-of-the-art baselines in demonstration tracking accuracy. Furthermore, we establish that providing such physically viable references to RL agents accelerates training convergence and enhances the final execution of agile and balancing behaviors. Source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [138] [Instrumentation for Imitation Learning: Enhancing Training Datasets for Clothes Hanger Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23847)
*Remko Proesmans,Thomas Lips,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出传感器集成(Instrumentation)策略，通过物体内嵌传感器提供状态信息，使机器人模仿学习更高效。在衣架插入任务中，仅用180次遥操作演示，利用传感器数据的扩散策略比纯视觉方法性能提升14-25%，且黑盒策略能自动优先利用传感器信号。通过专家策略rollout增强数据集，纯视觉学生策略可达到与传感器专家相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型行为模型在机器人操作领域取得进展，但数据需求过高阻碍了类似视觉语言模型的革命。现有方法缺乏高效的状态信息获取手段，导致学习效率低下。

Method: 在物体中集成传感器(Instrumentation)提供状态信息；利用180次遥操作演示训练扩散策略；通过instrumented expert policy的rollout数据增强遥操作数据集，训练视觉-only学生策略。

Result: 利用传感器数据的策略比纯视觉策略性能提升14-25%；黑盒模仿学习策略能自动优先利用传感器信号；增强数据集后，视觉-only学生策略达到与传感器专家相当的性能。

Conclusion: 传感器集成(Instrumentation)是一种增强机器人操作模仿学习的有效策略，可降低数据需求并提升性能。

Abstract: Large behaviour models have transformed the field of robotic manipulation, but prohibitive data requirements have thus far prevented a revolution similar to vision language models. We believe that instrumentation, i.e. sensor integration in objects, can provide invaluable state information and enable efficient learning for robotic manipulation. In this paper, we present instrumented imitation learning of clothes hanger insertion. Using 180 teleoperated demonstrations, we train diffusion policies with and without access to instrumentation data. Results show that policies leveraging instrumentation outperform vision-only counterparts by 14-25 %pt and exhibit greater task awareness. Crucially, a black-box imitation learning policy learns to prioritise instrumentation signals without explicit guidance. In addition, enhancing the teleoperation dataset with rollouts from an instrumented expert policy, enables a vision-only student policy to achieve performance comparable to the instrumented expert, thereby surpassing the original vision-only policy. These findings establish instrumentation as a promising strategy to enhance imitation learning for robotic manipulation. Datasets are available on Zenodo.

</details>


### [139] [Point Tracking Improves World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23856)
*Jiarui Guan,Wenshuai Zhao,Yue Pei,Ziliang Chen,Arno Solin,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出JOPAT，一种联合像素与2D点轨迹的世界-动作模型，通过扩散transformer同时预测潜在视觉观察、2D点轨迹和动作，在遮挡、离屏运动等长时序任务上优于纯像素基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级预测会混淆动力学与光照、纹理等无关因素，导致学到的表示对任务无关的视觉变化敏感，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 构建一个联合像素和2D点轨迹的世界-动作模型（JOPAT），使用单一的去噪扩散transformer同时预测潜在视觉观察、2D点轨迹（含可见性）和动作。轨迹提供显式的运动表示，在遮挡或部分离屏运动下保持鲁棒。

Result: 在LIBERO和真实世界LeRobot任务上，JOPAT优于基于像素的基线，在涉及遮挡、物体交互和离屏运动的长时序任务上提升最大。

Conclusion: 联合建模像素外观和显式运动轨迹比仅建模像素外观更有效，轨迹表示在长时序、遮挡和运动复杂场景中提供了更大的实用价值。

Abstract: Robot policy learning benefits from world-action models that capture environment dynamics, but pixel-level prediction entangles dynamics with nuisance factors such as lighting and texture, making learned representations vulnerable to task-irrelevant visual variation. We propose JOPAT, a JOint Pixel-And-Track World-Action Model that predicts latent visual observations, 2D point tracks with visibility, and actions in a single denoising diffusion transformer. The key insight is that tracks provide an explicit representation of motion that captures long-horizon dynamics and remains robust under occlusion or partial out-of-frame motion, offering greater utility than modeling pixel appearance alone. On LIBERO and real-world LeRobot tasks, JOPAT improves over pixel-based baselines, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks involving occlusion, object interaction, and off-screen motion.

</details>


### [140] [Robotic Strawberry Harvesting with Robust Vision and Deep Reinforcement Learning based Sim-to-Real Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23863)
*Al Bashir,Shao-Yang Chang,Partho Ghose,Prem Raj,Chen-Kang Huang,Azlan Zahid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套闭环机器人草莓采摘系统，融合了改进的YOLO26-seg视觉模块（HRAttnEdge-YOLO26-seg）和基于Isaac Lab仿真训练的PPO强化学习控制器，在温室实验中实现了84.3%的整体采摘成功率，证明了仿真训练DRL策略可替代传统规划器实现高效闭环采摘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低对硬件的依赖、减少开发成本，避免在真实部署前进行大量物理试验，同时解决传统基于逆运动学（IK）的规划器在复杂农业环境中运动不够平滑、适应性不足的问题。

Method: 1) 视觉模块：在YOLO26-seg基础上引入高分辨率P2分支、分割路径注意力和边缘监督原型学习，提升杂乱场景下的实例分割性能；2) 控制模块：在Isaac Lab中训练目标条件PPO策略，生成UR10e机械臂的平滑关节位置指令，并部署到真实机器人上执行采摘；3) 系统集成：基于ROS实现闭环感知-控制-执行。

Result: 视觉模型在自收集和公开数据集上分割性能提升10-14%；PPO控制器相比IK+MoveIt基线运动更稳定平滑；温室试验中采摘281颗草莓，达到96.6%到达成功率、91.3%抓取-拉取成功率和84.3%整体采摘成功率。

Conclusion: 任务特定感知结合仿真训练的PPO策略可作为传统规划器依赖型抓取操作的有效替代方案，在复杂农业环境中实现资源高效且可靠的闭环机器人采摘。

Abstract: This study presents a closed-loop robotic strawberry harvesting system that combines a robust vision module, simulation-trained deep reinforcement learning (DRL) control, and ROS-based realrobot execution. For perception, we propose HRAttnEdge-YOLO26-seg, a modified YOLO26-seg architecture that incorporates a high-resolution P2 branch, segmentation-path attention, and edgesupervised prototype learning to improve instance segmentation in cluttered scenes. For control, we train a target-conditioned Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) policy in Isaac Lab to produce smooth joint-position commands for a UR10e manipulator and deploy it on a UR10e robot for targetfruit reaching and harvesting. This simulation-based approach reduces hardware dependency, lowers development cost, and allows scalable policy training without exhaustive physical trials before real deployment. The proposed vision model demonstrated the highest overall performance among the evaluated methods. On both self-collected and public datasets, the model showed a 10 to 14% improvement in segmentation performance. In controlled in-house tests, the PPO controller produced stable and dynamically smoother motion than a inverse kinematics (IK)-based MoveIt baseline. In greenhouse trials, the proposed integrated system harvested 281 strawberries, achieving 96.6% reaching success, 91.3% grasp-and-pull success, and 84.3% overall harvesting success. These results illustrate that task-specific perception combined with simulation-trained PPO can serve as a practical and resource-efficient alternative to conventional planner-dependent reaching in manipulation, enabling reliable closed-loop robotic harvesting in complex agricultural environments.

</details>


### [141] [Autonomous Frontier-Based Exploration with VLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23165)
*Aarush Aitha,Avideh Zakhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用视觉语言模型(VLM)进行高层战略决策的自主探索管线，通过多模态提示（地图+视觉图像）选择最优前沿边界，在六个室内环境中覆盖率提升高达24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自主探索依赖几何启发式规则选择前沿边界，缺乏上下文空间推理能力。VLM的高级推理能力可以弥补这一不足，提升未知危险环境中的探索效率。

Method: 在决策点，机器人生成包含当前地图和潜在路径（前沿）视觉图像的多模态提示，VLM分析该提示选择最有希望的前沿边界，替代简单的几何启发式方法。高层VLM决策驱动低层传统机器人控制栈。

Result: 在六个室内环境仿真中，地图覆盖率相比现有方法提升高达24%。

Conclusion: 该方法轻量级、无需训练、易于迁移至任何配备标准传感器和互联网连接的机器人，验证了VLM在机器人自主探索中替代几何启发式的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic exploration of unknown and hazardous environments, a long-standing challenge, can be significantly improved by leveraging the advanced reasoning of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). We introduce a novel exploration pipeline where a VLM performs high-level strategic decision-making, guiding a conventional low-level robotics control stack. At decision points, the robot generates a multimodal prompt with its current map and visual imagery of potential paths, or frontiers. The VLM analyzes this prompt to select the most promising frontier, replacing simple geometric heuristics with contextual spatial reasoning. This approach, validated in simulation across six indoor environments, improves map coverage by up to 24\% over existing methods. Our pipeline is lightweight, training-free, and easily transferable to any robot with standard sensors and an internet connection.

</details>


### [142] [6G Communication Networks Enabling Embodied Agents: Architecture and Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23263)
*Lipeng Dai,Luping Xiang,Kun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向具身智能体的6G通信系统，提出了包含人体意图感知层、O-RAN传输层、智能中介层和具身层的分层通信架构，并通过端到端原型验证了毫秒级延迟与稳定闭环运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体对通信有更严苛和多样化的需求，但现有研究缺乏系统性地利用6G能力来支持具身智能体通信的方案。

Method: 从概念和工程两个角度研究6G赋能的具身智能体通信系统，首先厘清具身智能体与离身智能体的区别，分析具身智能体与6G的共生关系，提出分层通信架构，并基于5G O-RAN测试床、触觉设备、机械臂和中介平台构建端到端原型进行验证。

Result: 原型实现了毫秒级延迟和稳定闭环操作，验证了所提架构的可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层通信架构具实用性，可为未来6G-具身智能体研究与工业部署提供参考。

Abstract: Embodied agents, which couple intelligent decision-making with physical actuation in the real world, impose far more stringent and heterogeneous communication requirements than purely software-based agents. While 6G promises sub-millisecond latency, ultra-high reliability, native intelligence, and integrated sensing, systematic studies on how to exploit these capabilities for embodied agent communication remain limited. This article investigates 6G-enabled communication systems for embodied agents from both conceptual and engineering perspectives. First, we review the concept, embodiment value of embodied agents, and clarify their distinctions from disembodied agents. Then, we analyse the symbiotic relationship between embodied agents and 6G networks. We highlight how key 6G enablers can support the stringent requirements of human-robot interaction. Furthermore, we demonstrate the proactive role of embodied agents in bolstering communication networks through coverage extension, environmental sensing, and physical world understanding. Building on these insights, we propose a hierarchical communication architecture for human-robot remote interaction, comprising a human-intent perception layer, an open radio access network (O-RAN)-based transport layer, an intelligent intermediary layer, and an embodiment layer. To validate its feasibility, we implement an end-to-end prototype that integrates a haptic device, an industrial robotic arm, an intermediary platform, and a 5G O-RAN testbed. Experimental results demonstrate millisecond-level latency and stable closed-loop operation, confirming the practicality of the proposed architecture and providing a reference for future 6G-embodied agent research and industrial deployments.

</details>


### [143] [Sparse Compositional Flow Matching by geometric assembly from motion primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23341)
*Yan Tang,Yuanbo Tang,Tingyu Cao,Shaolun Huang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流匹配的组合式运动基元生成框架，通过运动基元字典学习和结构稀疏流匹配，直接在物理轨迹空间中进行组合生成，在Open X-Embodiment和3DMoTraj数据集上实现了SOTA性能，ADE提升19.2%，FDE提升21.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型将具身轨迹视为稠密、单一的整体信号逐点生成，拟合高维后验却忽略了数据的潜在结构，导致样本效率低下。作者认为轨迹具有组合式潜在结构，可分解为有限可复用的运动基元并与子任务边界对齐，因此需要一种能直接在物理空间组合基元的生成方法。

Method: 提出两个耦合设计：(1) 运动基元字典学习——为每个原子配备可学习的长度掩码和二进制起始指示器，使原子本身即为基元可原样复用；(2) 结构稀疏流匹配与几何约束——通过时长感知标记化生成二进制放置矩阵，并加入可微几何损失以保持相邻基元间的空间连续性和时间邻接性。

Result: 在Open X-Embodiment和3DMoTraj数据集上达到SOTA精度，FDE/ADE比率从1.8降至1.07，ADE提升19.2%，FDE提升21.0%。

Conclusion: 组合式潜在结构是具身轨迹生成的自然选择，通过在物理空间中直接组合运动基元，相比在隐空间中组合再解码的方法，能显著提升轨迹生成的精度和样本效率。

Abstract: Embodied trajectories, such as the executable motion sequences of robotic manipulators, underwater vehicles, and mobile robots, are a fundamental output of embodied AI. Modern generative models often treat them as a dense, monolithic signal generated point by point, fitting an intricate high-dimensional posterior while leaving the data's latent structure unmodeled, the same sample inefficiency long identified by the structured generative model literature. We argue that a compositional latent structure is a natural choice: many embodied tasks share recurring motion fragments that can be made explicit as a finite repertoire of reusable motion primitives, and compositional units naturally align with subtask boundaries to support task decomposition. Existing compositional generators, however, compose in a latent space and rely on post-hoc decoding to relate sampled units to actual trajectory segments. We instead compose directly in the physical trajectory space through a flow-matching framework with two coupled designs. Motion-Primitive Dictionary Learning equips each atom with a learnable length mask and binary starting indicators so the atom itself is the primitive, reused verbatim wherever it is placed. Structural Sparse Flow Matching with Geometric Constraints then generates a binary placement matrix using duration-aware tokenization and a differentiable geometric loss that enforces spatial continuity and temporal contiguity where adjacent primitives meet. On Open X-Embodiment and 3DMoTraj, the framework attains state-of-the-art accuracy and reduces the FDE/ADE ratio from 1.8 to 1.07, improving ADE by 19.2% and FDE by 21.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>
